```
def optimize(a, budget, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize(a)
    cur_score = score_fft(a)
    cur_smooth = None
    # precompute Lconv = next_fast_len(2n-1); Lg=next_fast_len(3n-2)
    beta = [5,10,20,40,80]
    eta_factors = [0.25,0.5,1,2,4]
    no_improve =0
    while time<deadline:
       C,D... maxD
       p = softmax(beta*(D-maxD))
       # optional top-mask: if p[max indices] not enough, add uniform small floor?
       G=grad_fft(p,a)
       H=G - np.mean(G)
       # smooth H? apply moving average kernel? Could do `H = 0.8*H + 0.2*np.convolve(H, np.ones(5)/5, 'same')` but np.convolve O(n) okay.
       scale = np.maximum(np.median(a),1e-15) / (np.max(np.abs(H))+1e-15)
       for base in [scale]:
          s_base = base*range? Actually base approx step to change by median a.
          for factor in [0.25,0.5,1,2,0.1,4]:
              s=factor*scale
              if s<=0: continue
              na = a - s*H
              na = np.clip(na, min_a, None)
              ssum=na.sum()
              if ssum<=0: continue
              na/=ssum
              sc=score_fft(na)
              if sc < cur_score - 1e-9:
                  a=na; cur_score=sc; improved=True; break
              # smooth accept?
          if improved: break
       if not improved:
           # add perturbation or change beta
    return a, cur_score
```

Convolution functions:
```
def _fft_conv_square(x):
    n=x.size
    L = next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    fx = _rfft(x, L)
    c = _irfft(fx*fx, n=2*n-1)
    c = np.real(c)
    np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    return c
```

```
   maxc = c.max()
   s = x.sum()
   return 2.0*n*maxc/(s*s), c # maybe not allocate c? For optimization need c/D.
```

Let's define:
```
def grad_conv(p, a, Lg):
    n = a.size
    fp = rfft(p, Lg)
    fa = rfft(a, Lg)
    # conv(p,a) = irfft(fp*fa,Lg), take [0:n]
    ca_pa = irfft(fp*fa, n=Lg)[:n]
    # conv(a, p_rev)
    pr = p[::-1]
    fpr = rfft(pr, Lg)
    ca_apr = irfft(fa*fpr, n=Lg)
```

```
def step_candidate(a, H, eta):
    med = np.median(a)
    # if med<=0 fallback
    r = H / (med + 1e-300)
    # clamp exponent
    e = -eta * r
    e = np.clip(e, -4.0, 4.0) # relative change e^4 ~54 max; safe but maybe too aggressive. Use 3.
    na = a * np.exp(e)
    # floor: na = np.maximum(na, 1e-15)
    ss=na.sum()
    if ss<=0: return None
    return na/ss
```

Let's draft code structure:

```
import numpy as np, time, math
try:
    from scipy import fft as sfft
    def _rfft(a, n): return sfft.rfft(a, n)
    def _irfft(a, n): return sfft.irfft(a, n)
    def _next_fast_len(m): return int(sfft.next_fast_len(m))
except ImportError:
    def _rfft(a,n): return np.fft.rfft(a,n)
    def _irfft(a,n): return np.fft.irfft(a,n)
    def _next_fast_len(m):
       L=1
       while L<m: L*=2
       return L
```
Actually numpy next fast power of 2; okay.

`normalize(v)`:
```
v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).copy()
if v.ndim!=1 or v.size==0: return None
v=np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
v=np.clip(v,0.0,1000.0)
s=v.sum()
if s<=1e-15: return None
v/=s
return v
```
But for parent, if sum huge, normalize. For floor min maybe.

`fft_c(v)`:
```
def square_conv(v, L=None):
   n=v.size
   if L is None: L=... 
   fa=_rfft(v, L)
   c=_irfft(fa*fa, n=2*n-1)
   c=np.ascontiguousarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
   # irfft returns real? It returns real array but maybe with tiny imag? _irfft returns real.
   np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
   return c
```
`irfft` with `n=2n-1`; we pass L as transform length, output length separate. Good.

`score_from_c(c,n,s=1): return 2*n*c.max()/s/s`
`score_fft(v,n,L): c=... s=v.sum(); return score, c`

`logsum_D(D,beta)`:
```
if beta < 1e-6: return D.mean()
maxD=D.max()
# if not finite, return inf
e=np.exp(beta*(D-maxD))
z=e.sum()
return maxD + math.log(z)/beta if z>0 else maxD
```
Could use `np.log(z)`; z ≤ lenD.

`softmax_D(D,beta)`:
```
maxD=D.max(); e=np.exp(beta*(D-maxD)); s=e.sum(); if s==0: p=np.zeros; p[argmax]=1
else p=e/s
return p
```

`grad_conv(p,a,Lg)`:
```
def grad_conv(p,a,Lg):
    n=a.size
    fp=_rfft(p,Lg)
    fa=_rfft(a,Lg)
    G=_irfft(fa*fp,n=Lg)[:n]
    fb=_rfft(a[::-1],Lg)
    G2=_irfft(fb*fp,n=Lg)[n-1:2*n-1]
    G += G2
    # ensure real float
    return np.asarray(G,dtype=np.float64)
```
Could `irfft` returns length Lg; if Lg not exactly? We passed n=Lg. Good.

Maybe p length 2n-1, a length n; Lg=next_fast(3n-2). For n=6962, 3n-2=20884, Lg=32768. conv indices up to 3n-3=20885 <Lg. Good.

`make_candidate(a,H,eta,alpha?)`: multiplicative:
```
def multiplicative_update(a,H,eta,floor_frac=1e-12, clamp=3.0):
    med=np.median(a)
    if med <=0: return None
    safe=np.maximum(a, floor_frac*med) # if med? maybe mean
    r=H/(med)
    e=-eta*r
    np.clip(e,-clamp,clamp,out=e)
    na=safe*np.exp(e)
    s=na.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return None
    na/=s
    return na
```
If a has zeros and med positive, floor small. Exponent clamp 3 gives factor 20. Good. Eta can be up to 1? If r=10, e=-30 clipped -3, factor0.05; high reduced; low with r=-10 clipped 3 factor20. OK.

Additive update:
```
def additive_update(a,H,scale):
    na=a - scale*H
    np.clip(na,0,None,out=na)
    s=na.sum(); if s<=0 return None
    return na/s
```
Scale = delta/maxabsH, delta=0.1*median.

Smoothing H:
```
def smooth_H(H):
   if len(H)<5: return H
   ker=np.array([0.1,0.2,0.25,0.2,0.1])
   Hs=np.convolve(H,ker,mode='same')
   return 0.7*H+0.3*Hs
```
`np.convolve` O(n) direct 5 taps, fast. This may help.

Pseudo:
```
def optimize(a, deadline, rng, target=1.5027):
    n=a.size
    a=normalize(a)
    Lconv=next_fast(2*n-1); Lg=next_fast(3*n-2)
    cur_a=a; c=...; cur_score=...
    best_a=cur_a.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    beta=10.0
    stale=0
    phase=0
    betas=[10,20,40,80,15,30,60,120,5]
    # start beta maybe 10
    while monotonic()<deadline:
       # compute current D if not have? store
       D = n*cur_c # cur_c from last score
       maxD=D.max()
       cur_log=logsum_D(D,beta)
       p=softmax_D(D,beta)
       # optional p floor: if maxp >0.5, add uniform? p = p + 1e-6/len; p/=sum
       G=grad_conv(p,cur_a,Lg)
       if not finite: break
       H=G - G.mean()
       H=smooth_H(H); H-=mean
       maxabs=np.max(np.abs(H)); if small: perturb
       improved=False
       # trial etas
       for eta in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.02,0.4,1.0,0.01]:
          na=mul(cur_a,H,eta)
          if na is None: continue
          cna=square_conv(na,Lconv); sca=2*n*cna.max() # sum normalized
          if not finite: continue
          sna_log=logsum_D(na? Dna=n*cna,beta)
          if sca < cur_score - 1e-10 or sna_log < cur_log - 1e-10:
             cur_a=na; cur_c=cna; cur_score=sca; cur_log=sna_log; improved=True
             if sca<best_score: best_score=sca; best_a=na.copy()
             break
       if not improved:
          # additive trials
          med=np.median(cur_a); delta=0.05*med
          scale=delta/(maxabs+eps)
          for s in [scale,0.5*scale,2*scale,0.2*scale,4*scale]:
             na=add(cur_a,H,s)
             ... if accept
       if not improved:
          stale+=1
          # random perturb to escape
          cand = perturb(cur_a, rng, 0.05+0.05*min(stale,5))
          cavy=square_conv(cand); scavy=...
          if scavy < cur_score - 1e-10 or scavy < best_score*1.02:
              cur_a=cand; cur_c=cavy; cur_score=scavy; stale=0
          else:
              # blend with best
              blend=0.7*cur_a+0.3*best_a; normalize
              cbl=...; if < cur_score: accept
          if stale>30:
              beta = betas[(betas.index(beta)+1)%len]
              stale=0
    return best_a,best_score
```

```
def perturb(a,rng,sigma):
   na=a.copy()
   # choose a few random blocks
   nb=rng.integers(1,5)
   for _ in range(nb):
       w=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//200), max(2,n//10)))
       s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w))
       fac=np.exp(png.normal(0,sigma))
       na[s:s+w]*=fac
   # small global noise
   na *= np.exp(png.normal(0,sigma*0.25,a.size))? Too expensive memory but fine 7k.
   # optional symmetric blend
   if rng.random()<0.3: na=0.5*(na+na[::-1])
   normalize
```

Pseudo with C/D storage:
```
def local_refine(a, deadline, rng, Lconv, Lg, target_score):
    n=a.size
    a=normalize(a)
    c=square_conv(a, Lconv)
    s=a.sum()
    score=2*n*c.max()/s/s
    D=c*(n/(s*s))
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    while time<deadline:
        # ensure cur_D finite
        beta = current beta
        p=softmax_D(D,beta)
        # if p norms: if p.max()>0.9, mix with uniform: p=0.9p+0.1/L?
        G=grad_conv(p,a,Lg)
        ... H smooth
        # candidate etas adaptive
        med=np.median(a); if med<=0: med=np.mean(a)
        rchar=np.median(np.abs(H))/med
        if rchar<1e-8: rchar=1e-8
        base_vals=[0.05/rchar,0.1/rchar,0.25/rchar,0.5/rchar,1.0/rchar,2.0/rchar]
        # filter finite and <=100
        cands=[]
        for eta in base_vals:
            if eta <=0 or eta>50: continue
            na=multiplicative_update(a,H,eta)
            if na is None: continue
            cna=square_conv(na,Lconv)
            sna=na.sum(); sc=2*n*cna.max()/(sna*sna)
            if not finite: continue
            Dna=cna*(n/(sna*sna))
            lg=logsum_D(Dna,beta)
            cands.append((lg,sc,na,cna,Dna))
        # choose best according to key maybe (score, log)
        if cands:
            # Sort by log primarily; but if one improves score, maybe choose best score among log improved?
            # We want both. Define rank = (lg, sc) lexicographic? Could choose candidate with minimum lg, even if score worsens within tolerance.
            bestc = min(cands, key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]))
            accept=False
            if bestc[1] < score - 1e-10: accept=True
            elif bestc[0] < log - 1e-10 and bestc[1] < score*1.05: accept=True
            if accept:
                a, c, D, score, log = bestc...
                if score<best_score: update
                stale=0
                continue
        # additive candidates if no accept
        ...
        if no accept:
            stale+=1
            # perturb
            cand=perturb_best(a, rng, sigma=0.03+0.03*min(stale,20))
            evaluate; if better exact/log accept
            # if stale>8: change beta (cycle)
            if stale % 5 ==0: beta = cycle
            if stale>50: a=best_a.copy() (reset)
```

Detailed code skeleton:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
try:
    from scipy import fft as _sfft
    def _rfft(x, n): return _sfft.rfft(x, n)
    def _irfft(x, n): return _sfft.irfft(x, n)
    def _next_fast_len(m):
        return int(_sfft.next_fast_len(int(m)))
except Exception:
    def _rfft(x,n): return np.fft.rfft(x,n)
    def _irfft(x,n): return np.fft.irfft(x,n)
    def _next_fast_len(m):
        L=1
        m=int(m)
        while L < m: L <<= 1
        return L

def _normalize(v): ...

def _score_c(c,n,s): ...
```
`_normalize` should output finite. For parent, if all zeros fallback. Use `np.nan_to_num` with `nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0`.

`_score_with_c(a,n,L)`:
```
def _score_with_c(a,n,L):
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300: return np.inf
    fa=_rfft(a,L)
    c=_irfft(fa*fa, n=2*n-1)
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    mx=float(c.max())
    score=2.0*n*mx/(s*s)
    return score, c, s
```
If a not finite, return inf.
But for many seeds, allocating c each. Good.

In optimizer, to avoid recomputing s for c if normalized, still.

`_softmax_D(D,beta)` and `_logsum_D`.
```
def _logsum_D(D,beta):
   if not np.isfinite(D).all(): return np.inf
   maxD=float(D.max())
   if beta <= 0: return maxD # or mean
   z=float(np.sum(np.exp(beta*(D - maxD))))
   if z <=0: return maxD
   return maxD + math.log(z)/beta
```
For beta large and D-max very negative, exp underflows to0; sum includes 1 at max. Good.

`_grad_conv`:
```
def _grad_conv(p,a,L):
    n=a.size
    fp=_rfft(p,L)
    fa=_rfft(a,L)
    g1=_irfft(fa*fp,n=L)[:n]
    fb=_rfft(a[::-1],L)
    g2=_irfft(fb*fp,n=L)[n-1:2*n-1]
    g=g1+g2
    return g.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
```
Maybe `irfft` returns length L but if L not int? fine.

`_mult_step`:
```
def _mult_step(a,H,eta,floor_frac=1e-12,clamp=5.0):
    med=float(np.median(a))
    if med <= 0: med=float(np.mean(a))
    if med <=0: return None
    safe=np.maximum(a, floor_frac*med)
    r=H/med
    e=-eta*r
    np.clip(e, -clamp, clamp, out=e)
    na=safe*np.exp(e)
    s=float(np.sum(na))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300: return None
    na /= s
    return na
```
If H contains NaN, e NaN; check finite before. Use `if not np.isfinite(H).all(): return None`? Could cost; do if np.isnan. Use `if not np.all(np.isfinite(H)): H=np.nan_to_num(H)`.

`_add_step`:
```
def _add_step(a,H,scale):
   na=a - scale*H
   np.clip(na,0.0,1000.0,out=na)
   s=na.sum(); if s<=... return None
   return na/s
```

`_smooth_H`:
```
def _smooth_H(H):
   if H.size < 5: return H
   ker=np.array([0.1,0.2,0.25,0.2,0.1])
   Hs=np.convolve(H,ker,mode='same')
   return 0.75*H + 0.25*Hs
```

`_perturb(a,rng,sigma)`:
```
def _perturb(a,rng,sigma):
   na=a.copy()
   n=na.size
   nblocks=int(rng.integers(1,4))
   for ...:
      w=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//500), max(2,n//8)))
      st=int(rng.integers(0,n-w))
      na[st:st+w]*=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
   # small global smoothing? 
   if rng.random()<0.2:
      na=0.5*(na+na[::-1])
   return _normalize(na)
```
Could add global multiplicative noise: `na *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma*0.1,na.size))` but memory okay. Use moderate.

`_coeff_alpha(n, alpha)`:
```
c=np.empty(n); c[0]=1.0
for k in range(n-1): c[k+1] = c[k]*(k+alpha)/(k+1.0)
return c
```
For n large and alpha small, c sum? okay.

```
   sc,c,s=_score_with_c(na,n,Lconv)

```

For exponent: use log to avoid overflow:
```
def exp_weighted(logb, t, q):
    w = logb + t*xm + q*xm*xm
    w = w - w.max()
    a=np.exp(w)
    return a
```
For uniform logb = zeros. For base coeff alpha, logb=np.log(base). For ramps, `a=(x+1e-3)**p` direct.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1300.0
    if budget > 60.0:
        deadline = start + max(1.0, budget - 20.0)
    else:
        deadline = start + max(0.2, budget * 0.9)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # parent
    parent = None
    N = 6962
    try:
        par = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if par.ndim == 1 and par.size > 0:
            par = np.nan_to_num(par, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            if par.size <= 20000 and np.isfinite(par).all():
                na = _normalize(par)
                if na is not None:
                    parent = na
                    N = int(na.size)
                else:
                    N = int(par.size) if par.size <= 20000 else 6962
            else:
                N = 6962
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        N = 6962
    if N < 1: N=1
    Lconv=...
    Lg=...
    # initial best
    best_score=np.inf; best_a=None
    def consider_initial(a):
       nonlocal best_score,best_a
       na=_normalize(a)
       if na is None: return
       sc,c,s=_score_with_c(na,N,Lconv)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score:
           best_score=sc; best_a=na
    if parent is not None:
       consider_initial(parent)
    # base minimal always
    if N == 1:
       base = np.ones(1)
    else:
       base = _coeff_alpha(N, 0.5)
       x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,N); xm=x-0.5
       # maybe base tilted t=1 for better
       w = 1.0*xm
       w -= w.max()
       base = base * np.exp(w)
    consider_initial(base)
    if best_a is None:
       best_a=np.ones(N,dtype=np.float64); best_a/=N
       best_score=_score_with_c(best_a,N,Lconv)[0]
    # if no time
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
       return final...
    # seed search if time
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 5.0:
       seed_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + min(90.0, 0.15*max(1.0,budget)))
       top = _seed_search(N, Lconv, parent, seed_deadline, rng)
       top.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
       if top:
          sc, a = top[0]
          if sc < best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_a=a
    # local refine
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        refine_end = deadline - (25.0 if deadline - time.monotonic() > 80.0 else 0.0)
        if refine_end <= time.monotonic(): refine_end = deadline
        best_a, best_score = _local_refine(best_a, refine_end, rng, N, Lconv, Lg, best_score)
    # second top maybe
    if 'top' in locals() and top and time.monotonic() < deadline - 40:
       # if top[0] maybe same; choose second non-similar? Use top[1]
       # set end = min(deadline-20, now + 60)
       end2 = min(deadline-20.0, time.monotonic() + 60.0)
       if end2 > time.monotonic():
           a2 = top[1][1].copy() if len(top)>1 else None
           if a2 is not None:
              a2b, sc2 = _local_refine(a2, end2, rng, N, Lconv, Lg, best_score)
              if sc2 < best_score: best_a, best_score = a2b, sc2
    # polish
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
       _polish(best_a, ...?)
```

```
def _polish(a, score, deadline, rng, n, Lconv):
    cur=a.copy(); cursc=score
    best=a.copy(); bestsc=score

       improved=False

          cand=_perturb(cur,rng,sig)
          sc,c,s=_score_with_c(cand,n,Lconv)

          if stale > 30:
             cur=best.copy(); stale=0
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
    return best,bestsc
```

```
def _local_refine(a, deadline, rng, n, Lconv, Lg):
    a=_normalize(a)
    if a is None: a=np.ones(n)/n
    if n <= 6:
       # quick random hill climb
       score,c,s=_score_with_c(a,n,Lconv)
       best=a.copy(); best_score=score
       while time.monotonic()<deadline:
           cand=_perturb(a,rng,0.2)
           sc,c2,s2=_score_with_c(cand,n,Lconv)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score:
              best_score=sc; best=cand; a=cand
       return best,best_score
    score,c,s=_score_with_c(a,n,Lconv)
    if not finite: score=2; c=zeros...
```

```
cur_a=a; cur_c=c; cur_s=s; cur_score=score; cur_D=cur_c*(n/(cur_s*cur_s))
best_a=cur_a.copy(); best_score=cur_score; best_c=cur_c; best_s=cur_s; best_D=cur_D
betas=(5,10,20,40,8,15,30,60,100); bidx=0
stale=0
while time.monotonic() < deadline:
    beta=betas[bidx]
    cur_log=_logsum_D(cur_D,beta)
    if not finite: break
    p=_softmax_D(cur_D,beta)
    # if p too spiky? add tiny uniform:
    p = p + 1e-8/cur_D.size; p/=p.sum()
    G=_grad_conv(p,cur_a,Lg)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
        G=np.nan_to_num(G)
    H=G - float(np.mean(G))
    H=_smooth_H(H)
    H -= float(np.mean(H))
    maxabs=float(np.max(np.abs(H)))
    if maxabs < 1e-18:
        stale +=1
    else:
       med=float(np.mean(cur_a)); if med<=0: med=1.0/n
       rchar=float(np.mean(np.abs(H)))/med
       if rchar<1e-12: rchar=1e-12
       etas = [0.03/rchar,0.08/rchar,0.2/rchar,0.5/rchar,1.0/rchar,2.0/rchar]
       # cap etas: if eta > 100 skip; min positive
       eta_list=[]
       for e in etas:
          if e > 0 and np.isfinite(e):
             eta_list.append(min(e,200.0))
       accepted=False
       # multiplicative
       best_key=None; best_tuple=None
       for eta in eta_list:
          na=_mult_step(cur_a,H,eta)
          if na is None: continue
          sc,cna,sna=_score_with_c(na,n,Lconv)
          if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
          Dna=cna*(n/(sna*sna))
          lg=_logsum_D(Dna,beta)
          ok = sc < cur_score -1e-10 or (lg < cur_log -1e-10 and sc < cur_score*1.03 + 1e-9)
          if not ok: continue
          key=(lg,sc)
          if best_key is None or key < best_key:
             best_key=key; best_tuple=(na,cna,sna,sc,Dna,lg)
       if best_tuple is not None:
          cur_a,cur_c,cur_s,cur_score,cur_D,_=best_tuple
          accepted=True
          if cur_score < best_score -1e-12:
             best_score=cur_score; best_a=cur_a.copy(); best_c=cur_c.copy(); best_D=cur_D.copy(); best_s=cur_s
          stale=0
          continue
       # additive
       delta=0.05*med
       base_scale=delta/maxabs
       best_key=None; best_tuple=None
       for factor in (0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0):
          s=base_scale*factor
          if s<=0 or not finite: continue
          na=cur_a - s*H
          np.clip(na,0.0,1000.0,out=na)
          tot=float(np.sum(na))
          if tot<=0: continue
          na/=tot
          sc,cna,sna=_score_with_c(na,n,Lconv)
          if not finite: continue
          Dna=cna*(n/(sna*sna))
          lg=_logsum_D(Dna,beta)
          ok=...
          if ok and key<... store
       if best_tuple is not None: accept as above; continue
       # no accepted
    stale += 1
    # perturb
    sig = 0.02 + 0.02*min(float(stale), 30.0)
    cand=_perturb(cur_a,rng,sig)
    if cand is not None:
       sc,candc,cands=_score_with_c(cand,n,Lconv)
       if np.isfinite(sc):
          Dcand=candc*(n/(cands*cands)); lg=_logsum_D(Dcand,beta)
          ok= sc < cur_score -1e-10 or (lg < cur_log -1e-10 and sc < cur_score*1.05+1e-9)
          if ok:
             cur_a,cur_c,cur_s,cur_score,cur_D,cand_log? = cand...; stale=0; if sc<best: update; continue
          elif sc < best_score -1e-10:

        stale=0

    if stale >= 24: # but stale reset above, so maybe if bidx changed but current best = original, need perturb. Use separate no_prog counter.
```

`_polish` code similar simple:
```
def _polish(a, score, deadline, rng, n, Lconv):
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score; cur_a=a.copy(); cur_score=score
    no=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        sigs=[0.02+0.01*min(no,20),0.05,0.1,0.2]
        accepted=False
        for sig in sigs:
            cand=_perturb(cur_a,rng,sig)
            if cand is None: continue
            sc,c,s=_score_with_c(cand,n,Lconv)
            if not finite: continue
            if sc < best_score -1e-10:
                best_score=sc; best_a=cand
                if sc < cur_score: cur_a=cand; cur_score=sc
                accepted=True; no=0; break
            elif sc < cur_score -1e-10:
                cur_a=cand; cur_score=sc; no=0; accepted=True; break
        if not accepted:
            no+=1
            if no > 20:
                cur_a=best_a.copy(); cur_score=best_score; no=0
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
    return best_a,best_score
```

```
def _exp_seq(logw):
   finite=np.isfinite(logw)
   if not np.all(finite): return None
   m=np.max(logw)
   if not finite: return None
   a=np.exp(logw-m)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0: return None
   return a/s
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy import fft as _sfft

    def _rfft(x, n):
        return _sfft.rfft(x, n)

    def _irfft(x, n):
        return _sfft.irfft(x, n)

    def _next_fast_len(m):
        return int(_sfft.next_fast_len(int(m)))

except Exception:
    def _rfft(x, n):
        return np.fft.rfft(x, n)

    def _irfft(x, n):
        return np.fft.irfft(x, n)

    def _next_fast_len(m):
        L = 1
        m = int(m)
        while L < m:
            L <<= 1
        return L


def _normalize(v):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    a /= s
    return a


def _square_score(a, n, L):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return np.inf, None, 0.0
        fa = _rfft(a, L)
        c = _irfft(fa * fa, n=2 * n - 1)
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
        np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
        mx = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(mx):
            return np.inf, c, s
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * mx / (s * s)
        return score, c, s
    except Exception:
        return np.inf, None, 0.0


def _logsum_D(D, beta):
    if D is None or D.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(D)):
        return np.inf
    maxD = float(np.max(D))
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        return maxD
    z = float(np.sum(np.exp(beta * (D - maxD))))
    if z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(z):
        return maxD
    return maxD + math.log(z) / beta


def _softmax_D(D, beta):
    if D is None or D.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    maxD = float(np.max(D))
    p = np.exp(beta * (D - maxD))
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.zeros_like(D)
        p[int(np.argmax(D))] = 1.0
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def _grad_conv(p, a, L):
    n = a.size
    fp = _rfft(p, L)
    fa = _rfft(a, L)
    g1 = _irfft(fa * fp, n=L)[:n]
    fb = _rfft(a[::-1], L)
    g2 = _irfft(fb * fp, n=L)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    g = np.asarray(g1, dtype=np.float64) + np.asarray(g2, dtype=np.float64)
    return g


def _smooth_H(H):
    H = np.asarray(H, dtype=np.float64)
    if H.size < 5:
        return H
    ker = np.array([0.1, 0.2, 0.25, 0.2, 0.1], dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        Hs = np.convolve(H, ker, mode="same")
        return 0.75 * H + 0.25 * Hs
    except Exception:
        return H


def _mult_step(a, H, eta, floor_frac=1e-12, clamp=5.0):
    if a is None or H is None:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(H)):
        H = np.nan_to_num(H, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    med = float(np.mean(a))
    if not np.isfinite(med) or med <= 0.0:
        med = 1.0 / max(1, a.size)
    safe = np.maximum(a, floor_frac * med)
    e = -eta * (H / med)
    np.clip(e, -clamp, clamp, out=e)
    na = safe * np.exp(e)
    s = float(np.sum(na))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    na /= s
    return na


def _perturb(a, rng, sigma):
    if a is None:
        return None
    try:
        na = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        n = na.size
        if n <= 0:
            return None
        nblocks = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        wmin = max(1, n // 500)
        wmax = max(wmin + 1, n // 8)
        for _ in range(nblocks):
            w = int(rng.integers(wmin, wmax))
            st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w)))
            na[st:st + w] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
        if rng.random() < 0.35:
            na = 0.5 * (na + na[::-1])
        na *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35 * sigma, na.size))
        return _normalize(na)
    except Exception:
        return None


def _coeff_alpha(n, alpha):
    c = np.empty(max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(c.size - 1):
        c[k + 1] = c[k] * (k + alpha) / (k + 1.0)
    return c


def _exp_seq(logw):
    if logw is None or logw.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(logw)):
        return None
    m = float(np.max(logw))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return None
    a = np.exp(logw - m)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    a /= s
    return a


def _seed_search(n, Lconv, parent, seed_deadline, rng):
    top = []

    def add(a):
        if time.monotonic() >= seed_deadline:
            return
        na = _normalize(a)
        if na is None:
            return
        sc, c, s = _square_score(na, n, Lconv)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            top.append((float(sc), na))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n) if n > 1 else np.zeros(n)
    xm = x - 0.5

    if parent is not None:
        add(parent)
    try:
        b05 = _coeff_alpha(n, 0.5)
        add(b05 * np.exp(1.0 * xm))
    except Exception:
        b05 = None
    add(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    alphas = (0.45, 0.5, 0.55)
    for alpha in alphas:
        try:
            base = _coeff_alpha(n, alpha)
            logb = np.log(base)
        except Exception:
            continue
        for t in np.arange(-2.0, 7.001, 0.5):
            a = _exp_seq(logb + t * xm)
            if a is not None:
                add(a)
            if time.monotonic() >= seed_deadline:
                break
        for t in np.arange(0.0, 6.001, 1.0):
            for q in (-1.0, 0.0, 1.0):
                a = _exp_seq(logb + t * xm + q * xm * xm)
                if a is not None:
                    add(a)
                if time.monotonic() >= seed_deadline:
                    break
            if time.monotonic() >= seed_deadline:
                break
        if time.monotonic() >= seed_deadline:
            break

    if b05 is not None:
        for lam in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0):
            add(b05 + lam * b05[::-1])
        for p in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0):
            add((x + 1e-5) ** p)
            add((1.0 - x + 1e-5) ** p)
        for _ in range(18):
            if time.monotonic() >= seed_deadline:
                break
            noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.28, n))
            add(b05 * noise)
            add(b05[::-1] * noise)

    try:
        top.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    except Exception:
        pass
    return top


def _local_refine(a, deadline, rng, n, Lconv, Lg):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
        a /= max(1, a.size)

    score, c, s = _square_score(a, n, Lconv)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        a = np.ones(max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
        a /= max(1, a.size)
        score, c, s = _square_score(a, n, Lconv)

    if n <= 6:
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_score = float(score)
        no = 0
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            cand = _perturb(best_a, rng, 0.35)
            if cand is None:
                no += 1
            else:
                sc, _, _ = _square_score(cand, n, Lconv)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-11:
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    best_a = cand
                    no = 0
                else:
                    no += 1
            if no > 80:
                break
        return best_a, float(best_score)

    cur_a = a.copy()
    cur_c = c
    cur_s = s
    cur_score = float(score)
    cur_D = cur_c * (float(n) / (cur_s * cur_s))

    best_a = cur_a.copy()
    best_c = cur_c.copy()
    best_s = cur_s
    best_score = cur_score
    best_D = cur_D.copy()

    betas = (5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 40.0, 8.0, 15.0, 30.0, 60.0, 100.0)
    bidx = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        beta = float(betas[bidx])
        cur_log = _logsum_D(cur_D, beta)
        if not np.isfinite(cur_log):
            break

        p = _softmax_D(cur_D, beta)
        p = p + (1e-9 / max(1, p.size))
        p /= float(np.sum(p))

        try:
            G = _grad_conv(p, cur_a, Lg)
        except Exception:
            G = None

        accepted = False
        if G is not None:
            G = np.asarray(G, dtype=np.float64)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
                G = np.nan_to_num(G, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            H = G - float(np.mean(G))
            H = _smooth_H(H)
            H -= float(np.mean(H))
            maxabs = float(np.max(np.abs(H))) if H.size else 0.0
            med = float(np.mean(cur_a))
            if not np.isfinite(med) or med <= 0.0:
                med = 1.0 / max(1, n)

            if maxabs > 1e-18:
                rchar = float(np.mean(np.abs(H))) / med
                if rchar <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(rchar):
                    rchar = 1e-3
                rchar = max(rchar, 1e-12)
                etas = (0.03 / rchar, 0.08 / rchar, 0.20 / rchar,
                        0.50 / rchar, 1.00 / rchar, 2.00 / rchar)
                eta_list = []
                for e in etas:
                    if np.isfinite(e) and e > 0.0:
                        eta_list.append(min(200.0, e))
                eta_list = sorted(set(eta_list), reverse=True)

                best_tuple = None
                best_key = None
                for eta in eta_list:
                    na = _mult_step(cur_a, H, eta)
                    if na is None:
                        continue
                    sc, cna, sna = _square_score(na, n, Lconv)
                    if not np.isfinite(sc):
                        continue
                    dna = cna * (float(n) / (sna * sna))
                    lgn = _logsum_D(dna, beta)
                    ok = (sc < cur_score - 1e-11) or (
                        np.isfinite(lgn) and lgn < cur_log - 1e-11 and
                        sc < cur_score * 1.03 + 1e-9
                    )
                    if not ok:
                        continue
                    key = (float(lgn), float(sc))
                    if best_key is None or key < best_key:
                        best_key = key
                        best_tuple = (na, cna, sna, float(sc), dna)
                if best_tuple is not None:
                    cur_a, cur_c, cur_s, cur_score, cur_D = best_tuple
                    accepted = True
                    if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = cur_score
                        best_a = cur_a.copy()
                        best_c = cur_c.copy()
                        best_s = cur_s
                        best_D = cur_D.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                    continue

                delta = 0.05 * med
                base_scale = delta / maxabs
                best_tuple = None
                best_key = None
                for fac in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
                    scle = base_scale * fac
                    if scle <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(scle):
                        continue
                    na = cur_a - scle * H
                    np.clip(na, 0.0, 1000.0, out=na)
                    ss = float(np.sum(na))
                    if ss <= 1e-300:
                        continue
                    na /= ss
                    sc, cna, sna = _square_score(na, n, Lconv)
                    if not np.isfinite(sc):
                        continue
                    dna = cna * (float(n) / (sna * sna))
                    lgn = _logsum_D(dna, beta)
                    ok = (sc < cur_score - 1e-11) or (
                        np.isfinite(lgn) and lgn < cur_log - 1e-11 and
                        sc < cur_score * 1.03 + 1e-9
                    )
                    if not ok:
                        continue
                    key = (float(lgn), float(sc))
                    if best_key is None or key < best_key:
                        best_key = key
                        best_tuple = (na, cna, sna, float(sc), dna)
                if best_tuple is not None:
                    cur_a, cur_c, cur_s, cur_score, cur_D = best_tuple
                    accepted = True
                    if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = cur_score
                        best_a = cur_a.copy()
                        best_c = cur_c.copy()
                        best_s = cur_s
                        best_D = cur_D.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                    continue

        if accepted:
            continue

        no_improve += 1
        sig = 0.02 + 0.015 * min(float(no_improve), 40.0)
        cand = _perturb(cur_a, rng, sig)
        cand_ok = False
        if cand is not None:
            sc, cna, sna = _square_score(cand, n, Lconv)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                dna = cna * (float(n) / (sna * sna))
                lgn = _logsum_D(dna, beta)
                ok = (sc < cur_score - 1e-11) or (
                    np.isfinite(lgn) and lgn < cur_log - 1e-11 and
                    sc < cur_score * 1.05 + 1e-9
                )
                if ok:
                    cur_a, cur_c, cur_s, cur_score, cur_D = cand, cna, sna, float(sc), dna
                    if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = cur_score
                        best_a = cur_a.copy()
                        best_c = cur_c.copy()
                        best_s = cur_s
                        best_D = cur_D.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                    cand_ok = True

        if not cand_ok:
            if no_improve >= 18:
                no_improve = 0
                bidx = (bidx + 1) % len(betas)
                cur_a = best_a.copy()
                cur_c = best_c
                cur_s = best_s
                cur_score = best_score
                cur_D = best_D.copy()


def _polish(a, score, deadline, rng, n, Lconv):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
        a /= max(1, a.size)
        score, _, _ = _square_score(a, n, Lconv)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(score)
    cur_a = a.copy()
    cur_score = float(score)
    no = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        improved = False
        for sigma in (0.02 + 0.01 * min(float(no), 30.0), 0.05, 0.1, 0.25):
            cand = _perturb(cur_a, rng, sigma)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            sc, _, _ = _square_score(cand, n, Lconv)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                continue
            if sc < best_score - 1e-11:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_a = cand
                if sc < cur_score:
                    cur_a = cand
                    cur_score = sc
                improved = True
                no = 0
                break
            if sc < cur_score - 1e-11:
                cur_a = cand
                cur_score = float(sc)
                improved = True
                no = 0
                break
        if not improved:
            no += 1
            if no > 35:
                cur_a = best_a.copy()
                cur_score = best_score
                no = 0
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1300.0
    if budget > 60.0:
        deadline = start + max(1.0, budget - 25.0)
    else:
        deadline = start + max(0.25, budget * 0.9)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    N = 6962
    try:
        par_raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if par_raw.ndim == 1 and par_raw.size > 0:
            par_raw = np.nan_to_num(par_raw, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            if par_raw.size <= 20000 and np.isfinite(par_raw).all():
                na = _normalize(par_raw)
                if na is not None:
                    parent = na
                    N = int(na.size)
                else:
                    N = int(par_raw.size)
            else:
                N = 6962
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        N = 6962

    if N < 1:
        N = 1
    if N > 20000:
        N = 6962
        parent = None

    Lconv = _next_fast_len(2 * N - 1)
    Lg = _next_fast_len(3 * N - 2) if N > 1 else 1

    best_a = None
    best_score = np.inf

    def consider_initial(a):
        nonlocal best_a, best_score
        na = _normalize(a)
        if na is None:
            return
        sc, _, _ = _square_score(na, N, Lconv)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = na

    if parent is not None:
        consider_initial(parent)

    try:
        base = _coeff_alpha(N, 0.5)
        if N > 1:
            xm = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N) - 0.5
            beta1 = 1.0 * xm
            beta1 -= float(np.max(beta1))
            base = base * np.exp(beta1)
        consider_initial(base)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)
        best_a /= float(N)
        sc, _, _ = _square_score(best_a, N, Lconv)
        best_score = float(sc) if np.isfinite(sc) else 2.0

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        seed_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + min(100.0, 0.18 * max(1.0, budget)))
        try:
            top = _seed_search(N, Lconv, parent, seed_deadline, rng)
        except Exception:
            top = []
        if top:
            sc0, a0 = top[0]
            if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc0)
                best_a = a0
    else:
        top = []

    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        refine_end = deadline
        if deadline - time.monotonic() > 90.0:
            refine_end = deadline - 35.0
        try:
            a_out, s_out = _local_refine(best_a, refine_end, rng, N, Lconv, Lg)
            if np.isfinite(s_out) and s_out < best_score:
                best_score = float(s_out)
                best_a = a_out
        except Exception:
            pass

    if top and len(top) > 1 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
        second_end = min(deadline - 20.0, time.monotonic() + 70.0)
        if second_end > time.monotonic():
            try:
                a2, s2 = _local_refine(top[1][1].copy(), second_end, rng, N, Lconv, Lg)
                if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                    best_score = float(s2)
                    best_a = a2
            except Exception:
                pass

    polish_end = deadline
    if polish_end - time.monotonic() > 0.5:
        try:
            a_out, s_out = _polish(best_a, best_score, polish_end, rng, N, Lconv)
            if np.isfinite(s_out) and s_out < best_score:
                best_score = float(s_out)
                best_a = a_out
        except Exception:
            pass

    try:
        final = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
        final = np.nan_to_num(final, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(np.sum(final))
        if s > 1e-12:
            final /= s
        final_list = [float(x) for x in final.tolist()]
    except Exception:
        final_list = [1.0 for _ in range(N)]

    if not all(np.isfinite(x) for x in final_list):
        final_list = [1.0 for _ in range(N)]

    try:
        _ = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
    except Exception:
        final_list = [1.0 for _ in range(N)]
        try:
            _ = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return final_list
```
